Let's think in discrete terms, maybe easier to use "copy" of a sequence with its reverse shifted to cancel? Nonnegative no cancel, but by arranging values we can make high values at positions that don't add to same sums. This is like placing weights on integers to minimize max pair-sum weight product. Another view: We can choose a_i; pair-sum coefficient C_k = ∑_{i+j=k} a_i a_j. If we take a_i roughly periodic by two sequences interleaved? For example partition indices into two sets A and B, assign high weights to A, low to B, with positions such that A+A, A+B, B+B sums spread. Uniform over all positions gives all counts. If high A and low B, C = high^2 A conv + low^2 B conv + 2 high low A*B. We want max of these. This resembles designing two sequences with low convolution. High part could be a Golomb/Sidon set, but then max self maybe? Let's explore.

Suppose we choose n large, let a_i = h on a set S of size m, l elsewhere (or zero) normalized. If l=0 (sparse high), sum = hm=1 -> h=1/m. Projected gradient with logsumexp:
def smooth_gradient(a, gamma):
  c=conv_fft(a)
  z = c * n perhaps. scale = c.max()
  logits = gamma * (z - z.max())
  w = exp(logits); wsum = w.sum(); if wsum small, use active top.
  g = zeros(n)
  For each k with w significant, g += w_k * grad_C_k(a,k)
  g /= wsum * ?
  Since c length 2n-1. We can loop over all k with nonzero weight (up to 9611). For each, gradient segment length up to n; total O(n * number active). If all k, O(n^2) too slow. Need use top-k or sparse weights. If gamma large, weights concentrated; use top K. For gamma moderate, weights spread but we can still top K e.g. 50/100. Average gradient over hot coefficients approximates. So top-K subgradient similar to previous but with weights. We can choose K=10-50. For n=4806, per iteration with K=20: conv FFT O(n logn) ~1e6, gradient 20*~2500 avg length=50k, projection O(n logn)=4806log, total maybe 0.005s? Could do thousands iterations in 2400s. Good.

- Maintain a (sum1). score known.
- For each iter:
  compute c, top_idx weighted by e^{gamma*(c-cmax)*n?}
  g = weighted avg grad
  Try step λ (initial maybe 0.1 or based on gradient norm). a_new=project_simplex(a - λ*g)
  score_new=fast_score(a_new)
  If score_new < score*(1-1e-12): accept; maybe λ*=1.1 (up to max); reset stall.
  Else reject; λ*=0.5; after several fails, try perturb/restart.

Let's refine projected gradient algorithm:
```
def optimize_simplex(a0, N, duration, rng, top_k_start=10, top_k_end=40, use_top=True, anneal=True):
    a = a0.copy(); a = normalize(a)
    score = fast_score(a)
    best = a.copy(); best_score=score
    step = 1.0  # scale? We'll use step parameter in terms of step * (1/n) maybe.
    t = 1; a_prev=a.copy()
    stuck=0
    it=0
    deadline=...
    while time<deadline:
       # top coefficients
       c=convolve_fft(a)
       # target top indices: pick K from coefficients with largest c, K increases
       if K >= len(c): idx=arange(len(c))
       else: idx=argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       # weights by temperature: gamma=?? Use z=n*c, maxz; w=exp(gamma*(z-maxz))
       # If gamma=0, weights=1.
       weight = ones; if gamma>0:
          z = N*c - N*c[idx].max()
          w = exp(min(0, z*gamma?)) / sum
       g = zeros(n)
       for k in idx: g += w_some * grad_C_k(a,k)
       g /= sumw
       # scale: g is O(1/n). We can set scale = N (so O1) and step ~0.05.
       g_scaled = N * g
       trial = project_simplex(a - step * g_scaled)
       new_score = fast_score(trial)
       if new_score < score*(1-1e-12) or (anneal and new_score < score + temp):
           a=trial; score=new_score; step = min(max_step, step*1.02)
       else:
           step*=0.5
           if step<1e-6: # restart
              a=best + noise; normalize; score=fast_score(a); step=init; t=1; a_prev=a
       if score < best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=score; stuck=0
       else: stuck++
       if stuck>...: perturb best and reset; also increase K/gamma.
       # momentum version instead of simple? 
```
But simple gradient with projected step may be slow; FISTA momentum can help. Previous FISTA used `y = a + momentum(a-a_prev)`, gradient at y, a=project(y-step*g), a_prev=a. It didn't do line search but periodic score. Could adapt. Simple line search maybe robust but less accelerated.

Let's outline code:
```
import numpy as np, time, math, sys

def project_simplex(v): ...
def normalize(a): s=a.sum(); return a/s if s>0 else uniform
def convolve_fft(a):
   n=len(a); m=1
   while m<2*n-1: m*=2
   F=np.fft.rfft(a,m); c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,m); return c[:2*n-1]
def fast_score(a):
   n=len(a); s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-15: return inf
   a=a/s
   c=convolve_fft(a)
   return float(2*n*c.max())
```
But in optimizer we need c and score. Let's have `score_and_conv(a)` returning (score,c,normalized a maybe). For trial, we can normalize? Project ensures sum1. Use c max.

```

# maybe sort? not needed

if temp>0:
   zmax = z.max()
   weights = np.exp(np.minimum(0, temp*(z - zmax))) # temp dimensionless e.g. 0-5
else weights=ones
```

Optimizer:
```
def optimize(state, budget, rng, base_step=0.05):
  a=normalize(state.copy()); score=fast_score(a); best=a.copy(); best_score=score
  a_prev=a.copy(); t=1.0; step=base_step; stall=0; it=0; K=8; temp=0.5; deadline=...
  while time<deadline:
     if decay temp: temp=... 
     K = int(min(c.size, 8 + 30*progress))
     t_new = 0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t)); momentum=(t-1)/t_new; t=t_new
     y=np.maximum(a + momentum*(a-a_prev),0); y=normalize(y) if sum>...
     c=convolve_fft(y); K...
     g=zeros; for idx weights: add 2*a_idx? gradient at y: use y not a.
     g *= len(y)  # scale
     trial = project_simplex(y - step*g)
     score_new = fast_score(trial)
     accept = score_new < score*(1-1e-12) or score_new < score + 0.02*exp(-it/2000)??
     if accept:
        a_prev=a; a=trial; score=score_new
        if score<best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=score; stall=0
        step=min(0.2, step*1.01)
     else:
        step*=0.7
        if step<1e-4:
           # perturb from best
           noise = rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n); a = best + noise; a=normalize(clip(a,0,None)); score=fast_score(a); a_prev=a.copy(); t=1; step=base_step; stall=0; K=8; temp=... 
     it+=1
     if it%50==0: progress print.
  return best,best_score
```

```
Detailed small optimizer:
```
def small_lse_solve(a0, m, duration, gamma=20.0, maxiter=500):
   # start u from log(a0) (with floor)
   u = np.where(a0>1e-12, np.log(np.clip(a0,1e-12,1)), 0)
   # normalize u - mean
   res = scipy.optimize.minimize(_lse_obj_grad, u, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12})
   a = softmax(res.x)
   return a
```

Precompute slices to speed:
```
def get_coeff_slices(m):
   slices=[]
   for k in range(2*m-1):
      lo=max(0,k-m+1); hi=min(m,k+1)
      idx = k - np.arange(lo,hi)
      slices.append((lo,hi,idx))
   return slices

Now, possible problem: L-BFGS-B objective/gradient using LSE may drive a to have very small values (softmax bounds) causing FFT precision but okay. Clip.

Let's sketch code for small LSE:
```
import scipy.optimize as sco

def softmax(u):
    u = np.clip(u, -50, 50)
    u -= u.max()
    z = np.exp(u)
    z /= z.sum()
    return z

def lse_score_grad(u, gamma, m):
    a = softmax(u)
    c = np.convolve(a,a)
    z = m * c
    zmax = z.max()
    logits = gamma * (z - zmax)
    w = np.exp(logits)
    wsum = w.sum()
    f = gamma * zmax + np.log(wsum)
    if not np.isfinite(f): return f, zeros
    w /= wsum
    g_a = np.zeros(m)
    slices = get_slices(m)
    # Only for weights > 1e-12
    for k in range(len(z)):
       wk = w[k]
       if wk > 1e-12:
          lo,hi,idx=slices[k]
          g_a[lo:hi] += (gamma * wk) * (2.0 * a[idx])
    mdot = np.dot(g_a, a)
    g_u = a * (g_a - mdot)
    return f, g_u
```
Here `gamma` can be passed. The constant `zmax` in objs; gradient w shifted. Multiplying by gamma. Good.

`scipy.optimize.minimize` may call with `args`. We can create top-level wrapper? Cannot use lambda/closure. We can define `def lse_minimize_func(u, gamma, m): return lse_score_grad(u,gamma,m)`. Then `sco.minimize(lse_minimize_func, u0, args=(gamma,m), jac=True, ...)`. This is allowed (no lambda).

```
def run_full(a0, N, duration, rng, init_step=0.05, max_step=0.25, k0=6, kmax=48, temp=0.0):
   deadline=...
   a=normalize(a0.copy()); score=fast_score(a); best=a.copy(); best_score=score
   a_prev=a.copy(); t=1; step=init_step; stuck=0; it=0; progress=0
   while time < deadline:
      K = int(min(2*N-1, k0 + (kmax-k0)*min(1, it/20000)))
      if temp increases slowly.
      # compute y
      t_old=t; t = (1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))/2
      mom=(t_old-1)/t
      y = a + mom*(a - a_prev); np.maximum(y,0); y=normalize(y)
      c=convolve_fft(y); score_y? not needed for gradient but maybe for acceptance? Use trial score.
      idx=top; weights...
      g=...
     oggles = maybe if g all zeros: break
      trial=project_simplex(y - step*g)
      sc=fast_score(trial)
      accept=False
      if sc < score*(1-1e-12): accept=True
      elif it < 5000 and sc < score + 0.03*(0.95**(it//100)): accept=True  # anneal
      if accept:
          a_prev = a
          a = trial
          score = sc
          if score < best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=score; stuck=0
          step = min(max_step, step*1.02)
      else:
          step *= 0.7
          if step < 1e-5:
             # reset with perturbation from best
             noise = rng.normal(0, 0.05/N, N) # small
             a = best + noise; np.maximum(a,0); a=normalize(a)
             score = fast_score(a)
             a_prev=a.copy(); t=1; step=init_step; stuck=0
          else:
             a_prev = a; t=1? # don't change a but reset momentum
             # maybe keep t? reset to1 to avoid bad extrapolation
             t=1.0
      it +=1
      stuck if not improved? best update. If stuck>5000: increase temperature/K? 
      if it % 1000 ==0 print.
   return best,best_score
```

```
def run_optimizer(a0, N, duration, rng, base_step=0.3, max_step=1.2, min_step=1e-5, Kmin=3, Kmax=48, verbose=True):
    a=normalize(a0); score=fast_score(a); best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    a_prev=a.copy(); t=1.0; step=base_step; it=0; reject_streak=0; no_improve=0; temp=0.02
    deadline=time.monotonic()+duration
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       frac = min(1.0, it/20000)
       K = int(min(2*N-1, Kmin + (Kmax-Kmin)*frac))
       # maybe K lower if early
       t_old=t; t=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t)); mom=(t_old-1)/t
       y = a + mom*(a-a_prev); np.maximum(y,0); ys=y.sum(); if ys<1e-12: y=a.copy(); else: y/=ys
       c=convolve_fft(y)
       g=active_gradient(y,c,K,temp_weight=0.5? maybe)
       # Optional: if g norm ==0 restart.
       trial=project_simplex(y - step*g)
       sc=fast_score(trial)
       accept = sc < score * (1 - 1e-12) or (temp>0 and sc < score + temp)
       if accept:
          a_prev = a
          a = trial
          score = sc
          if sc < best_score:
             best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; no_improve=0
          reject_streak=0
          step = min(max_step, step*1.01)
       else:
          reject_streak +=1
          step *=0.7
          if reject_streak>5 or step<min_step:
             # reset with perturbation
             sigma = 0.05 / np.sqrt(N) * (1 + np.log1p(it/1000)/2)
             noise = rng.normal(0, sigma, N)
             a = best + noise
             np.maximum(a,0, out=a)
             a = normalize(a)
             score=fast_score(a)
             a_prev=a.copy(); t=1; step=base_step; reject_streak=0
             no_improve=0
          else:
             t=1; a_prev=a  # keep current a
       temp *= max(0.999, 0.5**max(1,it/50000)?) 
       if sc < best_score? no? no_improve++? Actually if not improved but accepted, no_improve++.
       if no_improve>5000:
          # change strategy: K jump, step reset, perturb best larger
          a=...; K maybe
          no_improve=0
       it+=1
       if time since last_print >10: print...
    if score<best: update.
    return best, best_score
```

Let's draft code in my head.

```
"""
Search for nonnegative coefficients minimizing 2n max(conv(a))/(sum a)^2.
Uses small-scale smooth LSE optimization to find shapes, upsampling, and
projected-subgradient refinement on a large grid with stochastic restarts.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    import scipy.optimize as _sco
except Exception:
    _sco = None

_SECS_CACHE = {}
_SLICES_CACHE = {}
```

`get_coeff_slices(m)`:
```
def get_coeff_slices(m):
    if m in _SLICES_CACHE: return _SLICES_CACHE[m]
    slices=[]
    total=2*m-1
    for k in range(total):
        lo=max(0,k-m+1); hi=min(m,k+1)
        if lo<hi:
            idx=k - np.arange(lo,hi,dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            idx=np.array([],dtype=np.int64)
        slices.append((lo,hi,idx))
    _SLICES_CACHE[m]=slices
    return slices
```
For m up to 128, memory fine.

`softmax`:
```
def softmax(u):
   u=np.asarray(u,dtype=np.float64)
   u=np.clip(u,-50,50)
   uu=u-u.max()
   z=np.exp(uu)
   s=z.sum()
   if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): z=np.ones_like(u)
   z/=z.sum()
   return z
```

`lse_func(u, gamma, m)`:
```
def lse_func(u, gamma, m):
    a=softmax(u)
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    z=m*c
    zmax=float(z.max())
    vals=gamma*(z-zmax)
    # clip
    vals=np.clip(vals,-50,0)
    w=np.exp(vals)
    ws=w.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws<=0:
       return 0.0, np.zeros(m)
    f=gamma*zmax + math.log(ws)
    w /= ws
    g_a=np.zeros(m)
    slices=get_coeff_slices(m)
    # gamma factor outside
    for k in range(len(z)):
       wk=w[k]
       if wk > 1e-12:
          lo,hi,idx=slices[k]
          if lo<hi:
             g_a[lo:hi] += (gamma * wk) * (2.0 * a[idx])
    mval=float(np.dot(g_a,a))
    g_u=a*(g_a - mval)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g_u)): return f, np.zeros(m)
    return f, g_u
```
For m small, `np.convolve` direct O(m^2) fine. For gamma values, if gamma=0? LSE becomes constant? We won't use gamma=0. The objective f not used for compare; gradient okay.

Potential issue: L-BFGS-B may call with high u; softmax clips. Gradient when clipping active not exact (zeros); okay. Could set bounds? L-BFGS-B unbounded; clipping creates flat. Fine.

```
    deadline=time.monotonic()+duration
    best=[]
    seen=[]
    # unique initial u list
    def add_candidate(a): ... (top-level? no nested; can't nested? We can define top-level helper add? But for local logic no closure okay? We can just loop.)
```
No nested functions? The rule: "Make all helper functions top level and have no closures from function nesting. Don't use any lambda functions." It likely means don't define helper functions inside `propose_candidate`; but a small local function inside another may be considered nesting? To be safe, no nested functions at all. Use loops and top-level functions.

We can implement `run_small_lse(u0, gamma, m, maxiter)` top-level.
```
def run_small_lse(u0, gamma, m, maxiter=120):
    if _sco is None: return softmax(u0)
    try:
       res=_sco.minimize(lse_func, u0.copy(), args=(gamma,m), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                         options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxfun':maxiter*5})
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
          a=softmax(res.x)
          return a
    except Exception:
       return softmax(u0)
```
But if L-BFGS-B takes too long? maxfun limits.

`small_search`:
- Create init u list from initial profiles:
  top profiles as a candidates. `u=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1))`.
- For gamma in [10,30,80], for each u0 maybe, run if time left. But running all can be long. Use time check before each.
- Evaluate actual `fast_score` of result; keep best. Also keep original.
Return top few small profiles (a vectors). To avoid duplicate, compare score.

Since no nested, we can just append to list.

```
def small_search(m, profiles, duration):
   results=[]
   deadline=time.monotonic()+duration
   # unique profiles: use length m
   u0_list=[]
   profs_unique=[]
   for a in profiles:
      if len(a)!=m: a=upsample_profile(a,m)
      sc=fast_score(a)
      # add if score < global? up to 6
      if len(profs_unique)<8:
         profs_unique.append((a,sc)); u0_list.append(np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1.0)))
   # add a few random/trend
   rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
   for ...:
   best_a=...; best_sc=min
   gammas=[10,30,80]
   for gamma in gammas:
      for u0 in u0_list:
         if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
         a=run_small_lse(u0,gamma,m, maxiter=80)
         sc=fast_score(a); update results
   return best_a,best_sc
```
`profis` passed likely all candidates. But we don't want small_search to spend too much before candidate eval. We'll call after quick candidates with duration 250. `profiles` maybe top 5 initial + analytic.

Now full candidate profiles. We'll define functions:
- `profile_uniform(N)`

`upsample_profile` use log interpolation:
```
def upsample_profile(profile, N):
   p=np.asarray(profile,float); p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(N)/N
   p=p/s
   if len(p)==N: return p
   m=len(p)
   u=np.log(np.clip(p,1e-15,1.0))
   x0=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   x1=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N
   u1=np.interp(x1,x0,u)
   a=np.exp(u1-u1.max()); a=a/a.sum(); return a
```
If profile has zeros, floor. Good.

```
def profile_params(v, m):
   x=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   u = np.zeros(m)
   if len(v)>0: u += v[0]*np.sin(np.pi*(x-0.5))
   if len(v)>1: u += v[1]*np.cos(np.pi*x)
   if len(v)>2: u += v[2]*np.sin(2*np.pi*(x-0.5))
   if len(v)>3: u += v[3]*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
   if len(v)>4: u += v[4]*np.sin(3*np.pi*(x-0.5))
   if len(v)>5: u += v[5]*np.cos(3*np.pi*x)
   if len(v)>6: u += v[6]*(x-0.5)
   if len(v)>7: u += v[7]*(x*(1-x)-1/12) # centered
   # maybe subtract mean to fix normalization? softmax invariant, not needed.
   return softmax(u)
```

`run_optimizer` details: The active gradient uses c of y. But score for acceptance computed on trial; if trial sum not exactly1 due projection numeric, fast_score normalizes but we can trust. The best kept normalized.

Let's code `run_optimizer` top-level:
```
def run_optimizer(a0, duration, rng, base_step=0.3, max_step=1.5, min_step=1e-5, Kmin=3, Kmax=48, temp_init=0.02):
    a=normalize(a0.copy())
    score=fast_score(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    a_prev=a.copy(); t=1.0; step=base_step
    it=0; reject=0; no_improve=0; temp=temp_init
    deadline=time.monotonic()+duration
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       if no_improve > 8000 and step > min_step*10:
           # more aggressive restart
           sigma = 0.1/np.sqrt(len(a))
           a = best + rng.normal(0,sigma,len(a)); a=np.maximum(a,0); a=normalize(a)
           score=fast_score(a); a_prev=a.copy(); t=1; step=base_step; no_improve=0
       frac = min(1.0, it/20000)
       K = int(min(2*len(a)-1, Kmin + (Kmax-Kmin)*frac))
       t_old=t
       t = 0.5*(1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
       mom = (t_old-1.0)/t
       y = a + mom*(a - a_prev)
       np.maximum(y,0.0,out=y)
       ys=y.sum()
       if ys<1e-12:
           y=a.copy()
       else:
           y = y/ys
       c=convolve_fft(y)
       g=active_gradient(y,c,K,0.5+1.5*frac)
       if not np.any(g):
           step=base_step; y=a.copy()
       trial=project_simplex(y - step*g)
       sc=fast_score(trial)
       accept = (sc < score*(1.0-1e-12))
       if not accept and temp>1e-8:
           accept = sc < score + temp
       if accept:
           a_prev = a
           a = trial
           score = sc
           if sc < best_score:
               best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; no_improve=0
               reject=0
           else:
               no_improve += 1
           step = min(max_step, step*1.01)
           temp *= 0.9995
       else:
           reject +=1
           no_improve +=1
           step *= 0.7
           if step < min_step or reject > 6:
               # initialize from best with noise
               sigma = (0.05 + 0.05*frac)/max(1.0, np.sqrt(len(a)))
               a = best + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, len(a))
               np.maximum(a,0.0,out=a)
               a=normalize(a)
               score=fast_score(a)
               a_prev=a.copy(); t=1.0; step=base_step; reject=0
           else:
               # keep a; reset momentum to avoid bad extrapolation
               a_prev=a.copy(); t=1.0
       it +=1
       if it % 2000 ==0:
           if time.monotonic()-last_print>10:
               print(f"    opt it={it} score={best_score:.7f} cur={score:.7f} step={step:.5f} K={K}")
               last_print=time.monotonic()
    if score < best_score:
       best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    return best, best_score
```
Potential: `active_gradient` returns g with average w sum1. Good.

`active_gradient` uses temp coefficient: `temp_coeff` dimensionless. Let's implement:
```
def active_gradient(a, c, K, temp_coeff=0.0):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    if K>=L:
       idx=np.arange(L); weights=np.full(L,1.0/L)
    else:
       if K<=0: return zeros
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       # If all c equal, okay
       if temp_coeff>0:
           z = c[idx] * n
           zmax=z.max()
           rel=z-zmax
           weights=np.exp(np.clip(temp_coeff*rel, -50.0, 0.0))
           ws=weights.sum()
           if ws>0: weights/=ws
           else: weights=np.full(K,1/K)
       else:
           weights=np.full(K,1/K)
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for k,wk in zip(idx,weights):
       if wk <=0: continue
       k=int(k)
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n,k+1)
       if lo<hi:
           idx2 = k - np.arange(lo,hi,dtype=np.int64)
           g[lo:hi] += (2.0*wk)*a[idx2]
    return g
```
If temp_coeff large, weights may underflow to one. Clip to 0 max relative. Good.

Potential issue: `proj_simplex` of y - step*g with step*g small but due y normalized. Project returns sum1. However if y has many zeros, projection maybe. Good.

Let's ensure `project_simplex` stable:
```
def project_simplex(v):
   n=len(v)
   if n==0: return v.copy()
   u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
   cssv=np.cumsum(u)
   cond = u*(np.arange(1,n+1, dtype=np.float64)) > (cssv - 1.0)
   if not cond.any():
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   rho=int(np.where(cond)[0][-1])
   theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
   w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
   s=w.sum()
   if s>0: w /= s # slight correction
   return w
```
The standard projection already sum1. Adding correction okay.

```
def choose_top(cands, k):
    sorted_c=sorted(cands) # no key; tuple (score,name,a) comparison if scores equal then name, okay.
    out=[]
    for item in sorted_c:
       if not np.isfinite(item[0]): continue
       if len(out)>=k: break
       # uniqueness: for existing if norm diff < 0.02 skip
       duplicate=False
       for _,_,b in out:
          if len(item[2])==len(b):
             if np.linalg.norm(item[2]-b) < 0.02: duplicate=True; break
       if not duplicate: out.append(item)
    return out
```

Sanitize sequence before return: convert to list floats, clip nonfinite. If sum <0.01, maybe scale to sum1. Use:
```
def finalize_sequence(seq):
   arr=np.asarray(seq,float)
   arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s < 0.01:
      arr = np.ones_like(arr) if len(arr)>0 else np.array([1.0])
      s=arr.sum()
   arr = arr / s  # keep sum1 (optional)
   return [float(v) for v in arr]
```

```
def profile_random_smooth(rng, n, nsmooth=3, kernel=5):
   v=rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,n)
   k=np.ones(kernel)/kernel
   for _ in range(nsmooth):
      v=np.convolve(v,k,'same')
   return normalize(v)
```

`choose_top`: sort by key. Use `_candidate_key`.
```
def choose_top(cands, k):
    if not cands: return []
    s=sorted(cands, key=_candidate_key)
    res=[]
    for item in s:
       sc=item[0]
       if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
       a=item[2]
       dup=False
       for _,_,b in res:
          if len(a)==len(b):
             # cheap: if max abs diff <1e-3 skip
             if np.max(np.abs(a-b)) < 1e-3:
                dup=True; break
       if not dup:
          res.append(item)
          if len(res)>=k: break
    return res
```
Use max abs diff rather than norm for speed; fine.

```
def small_refine(profiles, m, duration):
    deadline=time.monotonic()+duration
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    if _sco is None:
       for a in profiles: sc=fast_score(a); update
       return best_a,best_sc
    u_list=[]
    # unique profiles
    for a in profiles:
       if a is None: continue
       a=normalize(a)
       sc=fast_score(a); update
       if a.size != m: a=upsample_profile(a,m)
       u=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1.0)); u=u-u.mean()
       # unique by max diff
       dup=False ...
       if not dup: u_list.append(u); if len>8 break
    # add deterministic
    x=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    u_list.append(1.0*(x-0.5))
    u_list.append(1.0*np.sin(np.pi*(x-0.5)))
    u_list.append(1.5*np.tanh(4.0*(x-0.5)))
    # random? rng fixed
    rng=np.random.default_rng(99)
    u_list.append(rng.normal(0,0.5,m))
    j=0
    for gamma in (10.0,30.0,80.0):
       for u0 in u_list:
          if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
          maxit=70
          a=run_small_lse(u0,gamma,m,maxit)
          sc=fast_score(a); update
          # also run with maxit 120 for best after gamma 30? maybe
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Now, let's detail `convolve_sequence`:
```
def convolve_sequence(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n<=2000:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
       size=1
       need=2*n-1
       while size<need: size <<=1
       f=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
       c=np.fft.irfft(f*f, size)[:need]
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    c[np.isnan(c)] = 0? Better `c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)` then max(0).
    c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
If `a` contains inf? We sanitize. Good.

`normalize`:
```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/max(1,a.size))
    return a/s
```

```
def fast_score(a):
   arr=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if arr.size==0: return inf
   arr=np.clip(np.nan_to_num(arr),0,None)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s<=1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s): return inf
   arr=arr/s
   return score_normalized(arr)
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a)**2)

The program combines:
  * fast FFT-based scoring,
  * a small-scale smooth optimization in a flexible positive basis,
  * differential evolution over a low-dimensional positive profile,
  * upsampled profile candidates,
  * projected-subgradient/FISTA-style refinement with stochastic restarts
    on a length-8192 grid.

All returned entries are nonnegative floats, and the sequence is normalized
so its sum is 1 (the objective is scale-invariant).
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _sco
except Exception:
    _sco = None


_CUR_SPARAM_M = 0
_PARAM_BOUNDS = [(-2.0, 2.0)] * 8
_SLICES_CACHE = {}


def _candidate_key(item):
    return item[0]


def get_initial_sequence():
    try:
        g = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if g is None or g.size == 0:
        return None
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    if g.sum() <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return g


def normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = convolve_sequence(a)
    sc = float(2.0 * len(a) * c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return float("inf")
    return sc


def score_normalized(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s) or abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-8:
        a = normalize(a)
        if a.sum() <= 1e-12:
            return float("inf")
    c = convolve_sequence(a)
    sc = float(2.0 * len(a) * c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return float("inf")
    return sc


def convolve_sequence(a):
    n = len(a)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 2000:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        size = 1
        while size < need:
            size <<= 1
        f = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
        c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, size)[:need]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def project_simplex(v):
    n = len(v)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v, dtype=np.float64)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * idx > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    rho = int(np.where(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    w = w / s
    return w


def upsample_profile(profile, n):
    p = np.asarray(profile, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    p = p / s
    if p.size == n:
        return p
    m = p.size
    u = np.log(np.clip(p, 1e-15, 1.0))
    x0 = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    x1 = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    u1 = np.interp(x1, x0, u)
    a = np.exp(u1 - u1.max())
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def add_candidate(out, name, a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return
    a = a / s
    sc = score_normalized(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        out.append((sc, name, a))


def profile_uniform(n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)


def profile_sin(n, amp):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    p = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(np.pi * (x - 0.5))
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-9)
    return p


def profile_logistic(n, a, b):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    p = 1.0 + float(a) * np.tanh(float(b) * (x - 0.5))
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-9)
    return p


def profile_power(n, a, b):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    p = np.power(x, float(a)) * np.power(1.0 - x, float(b))
    p = np.where(p > 1e-12, p, 1e-12)
    return p


def profile_step(n, frac, delta):
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(round(float(frac) * float(n)))
    k = max(1, min(n - 1, k))
    high = 1.0 + float(delta)
    low = max(1e-6, 1.0 - float(delta))
    p[:k] = high
    p[k:] = low
    return p


def profile_random_smooth(rng, n):
    v = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, n)
    kern = np.ones(5, dtype=np.float64) / 5.0
    for _ in range(3):
        v = np.convolve(v, kern, "same")
    return v


def make_candidates(n, given, rng, out):
    add_candidate(out, "uniform", profile_uniform(n))

    if given is not None:
        add_candidate(out, "given", upsample_profile(given, n))

    for amp in (0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0):
        add_candidate(out, "sin_%s" % amp, profile_sin(n, amp))

    logistic_specs = (
        (0.3, 2.0), (0.4, 2.0), (0.4, 4.0), (0.6, 2.0),
        (0.6, 4.0), (0.8, 2.0), (0.8, 6.0), (1.0, 4.0), (1.0, 8.0)
    )
    for a, b in logistic_specs:
        add_candidate(out, "log_%s_%s" % (a, b), profile_logistic(n, a, b))

    power_specs = (
        (0.5, 0.0), (0.0, 0.5), (1.0, 0.0), (0.0, 1.0),
        (0.5, 0.5), (2.0, 0.0), (0.0, 2.0), (0.5, 1.0), (1.0, 2.0)
    )
    for a, b in power_specs:
        add_candidate(out, "pow_%s_%s" % (a, b), profile_power(n, a, b))

    step_specs = (
        (0.25, 0.3), (0.33, 0.3), (0.5, 0.2),
        (0.5, 0.33), (0.5, 0.6), (0.5, 1.0), (0.67, 0.3)
    )
    for f, d in step_specs:
        add_candidate(out, "step_%s_%s" % (f, d), profile_step(n, f, d))

    for i in range(3):
        add_candidate(out, "rand_%d" % i, profile_random_smooth(rng, n))


def choose_top(cands, k):
    if not cands:
        return []
    s = sorted(cands, key=_candidate_key)
    res = []
    for item in s:
        if not np.isfinite(item[0]):
            continue
        a = item[2]
        dup = False
        for existing in res:
            b = existing[2]
            if len(a) == len(b):
                if np.max(np.abs(a - b)) < 1e-4:
                    dup = True
                    break
        if not dup:
            res.append(item)
            if len(res) >= k:
                break
    return res


def append_unique_start(res, item):
    if item is None:
        return
    a = item[2]
    for existing in res:
        b = existing[2]
        if len(a) == len(b) and np.max(np.abs(a - b)) < 0.04:
            return
    if len(res) < 5:
        res.append(item)


def softmax(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.clip(u, -50.0, 50.0)
    u = u - u.max()
    z = np.exp(u)
    s = z.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
        z = np.ones_like(u)
    return z / z.sum()


def get_coeff_slices(m):
    if m in _SLICES_CACHE:
        return _SLICES_CACHE[m]
    slices = []
    total = 2 * m - 1
    for k in range(total):
        lo = max(0, k - m + 1)
        hi = min(m, k + 1)
        if lo < hi:
            idx = k - np.arange(lo, hi, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            idx = np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
        slices.append((lo, hi, idx))
    _SLICES_CACHE[m] = slices
    return slices


def profile_params(v, m):
    x = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    u = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size > 0:
        u += v[0] * np.sin(np.pi * (x - 0.5))
    if v.size > 1:
        u += v[1] * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    if v.size > 2:
        u += v[2] * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (x - 0.5))
    if v.size > 3:
        u += v[3] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    if v.size > 4:
        u += v[4] * np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * (x - 0.5))
    if v.size > 5:
        u += v[5] * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)
    if v.size > 6:
        u += v[6] * (x - 0.5)
    if v.size > 7:
        u += v[7] * (x * (1.0 - x) - 1.0 / 12.0)
    return softmax(u)


def param_objective(v):
    m = int(_CUR_SPARAM_M)
    if m <= 0:
        return 1.0e12
    a = profile_params(v, m)
    sc = fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1.0e12
    return sc


def de_shape_search(m, duration, seed):
    global _CUR_SPARAM_M
    if _sco is None or duration < 20 or m <= 0:
        return None
    _CUR_SPARAM_M = m
    maxiter = max(5, min(45, int(duration // 8)))
    try:
        try:
            res = _sco.differential_evolution(
                param_objective,
                _PARAM_BOUNDS,
                maxiter=maxiter,
                polish=False,
                tol=1.0e-4,
                popsize=12,
                seed=seed,
                workers=1
            )
        except TypeError:
            res = _sco.differential_evolution(
                param_objective,
                _PARAM_BOUNDS,
                maxiter=maxiter,
                polish=False,
                tol=1.0e-4,
                popsize=12,
                seed=seed
            )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            a = profile_params(res.x, m)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(a)) and a.sum() > 1e-12:
                return a
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def lse_objective(u, gamma, m):
    a = softmax(u)
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    z = float(m) * c
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    vals = gamma * (z - zmax)
    vals = np.clip(vals, -50.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(vals)
    ws = w.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    f = float(gamma * zmax + math.log(ws))
    w = w / ws
    g_a = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    slices = get_coeff_slices(m)
    for k in range(len(z)):
        wk = w[k]
        if wk <= 1.0e-14:
            continue
        lo, hi, idx = slices[k]
        if lo < hi:
            g_a[lo:hi] += (gamma * wk) * (2.0 * a[idx])
    mval = float(np.dot(g_a, a))
    g_u = a * (g_a - mval)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g_u)):
        g_u = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    return f, g_u


def run_small_lse(u0, gamma, m, maxiter):
    u0 = np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if _sco is None:
        return softmax(u0)
    try:
        res = _sco.minimize(
            lse_objective,
            u0.copy(),
            args=(float(gamma), int(m)),
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "ftol": 1.0e-12,
                "gtol": 1.0e-6,
                "maxfun": max(200, 4 * int(maxiter))
            }
        )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            a = softmax(res.x)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(a)) and a.sum() > 1e-12:
                return a
    except Exception:
        pass
    return softmax(u0)


def small_refine(profiles, m, duration):
    if m <= 0 or duration <= 5:
        best_a = None
        best_sc = float("inf")
        if profiles:
            for a in profiles:
                if a is None:
                    continue
                a = normalize(a)
                sc = score_normalized(a)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
        return best_a, best_sc

    best_a = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    def updatescores(a, sc):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        if a is None or sc >= best_sc or not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_sc = float(sc)

    if profiles:
        for a in profiles:
            if a is None:
                continue
            a = normalize(a)
            sc = score_normalized(a)
            update(a, sc)

    u_list = []
    if profiles:
        for a in profiles[:8]:
            if a is None:
                continue
            a = normalize(a)
            if a.size != m:
                a = upsample_profile(a, m)
            u = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1.0))
            u = u - float(u.mean())
            dup = False
            for prev in u_list:
                if np.max(np.abs(u - prev)) < 0.05:
                    dup = True
                    break
            if not dup:
                u_list.append(u)
            if len(u_list) >= 8:
                break

    x = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    u_list.append(1.0 * (x - 0.5))
    u_list.append(1.0 * np.sin(np.pi * (x - 0.5)))
    u_list.append(1.5 * np.tanh(4.0 * (x - 0.5)))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(555)
    u_list.append(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, m))

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, duration)

    if _sco is not None:
        gammas = (10.0, 30.0, 80.0)
        maxits = (70, 80, 80)
        for gamma, mx in zip(gammas, maxits):
            for u0 in u_list:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                a = run_small_lse(u0, gamma, m, mx)
                sc = score_normalized(a)
                update(a, sc)

    if best_a is None and u_list:
        best_a = softmax(u_list[0])
        best_sc = score_normalized(best_a)

    return best_a, best_sc


def active_gradient(a, c, k_count, temp_coeff):
    n = len(a)
    total = 2 * n - 1
    if k_count <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if k_count >= total:
        idx = np.arange(total, dtype=np.int64)
        weights = np.full(total, 1.0 / float(total), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -k_count)[-k_count:]
        if temp_coeff > 1e-12:
            z = c[idx] * float(n)
            zmax = float(np.max(z))
            rel = z - zmax
            weights = np.exp(np.clip(float(temp_coeff) * rel, -50.0, 0.0))
            ws = weights.sum()
            if ws > 1e-300:
                weights = weights / ws
            else:
                weights = np.full(k_count, 1.0 / float(k_count), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            weights = np.full(k_count, 1.0 / float(k_count), dtype=np.float64)

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for kk in range(len(idx)):
        wk = float(weights[kk])
        if wk <= 1e-300:
            continue
        k = int(idx[kk])
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n, k + 1)
        if lo < hi:
            idx2 = k - np.arange(lo, hi, dtype=np.int64)
            g[lo:hi] += (2.0 * wk) * a[idx2]
    return g


def run_optimizer(a0, duration, rng, base_step, max_step, k_min, k_max):
    a = normalize(a0.copy())
    score = score_normalized(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / float(len(a))
        score = score_normalized(a)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    a_prev = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    step = float(base_step)
    it = 0
    rejects = 0
    no_improve = 0
    temp = 0.015
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, duration)
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    n = len(a)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if no_improve > 8000:
            sigma = (0.05 + 0.015 * min(5.0, float(no_improve) / 1000.0)) / max(1.0, math.sqrt(float(n)))
            perturbed = best_a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            perturbed = np.maximum(perturbed, 0.0)
            perturbed = normalize(perturbed)
            new_score = score_normalized(perturbed)
            if np.isfinite(new_score):
                a = perturbed
                score = new_score
            a_prev = a.copy()
            t = 1.0
            step = float(base_step)
            no_improve = 0

        frac = min(1.0, float(it) / 20000.0)
        k_count = int(min(2 * n - 1, k_min + (k_max - k_min) * frac))
        if k_count < 1:
            k_count = 1

        t_old = t
        t = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        mom = (t_old - 1.0) / t
        y = a + mom * (a - a_prev)
        np.maximum(y, 0.0, out=y)
        ys = y.sum()
        if ys <= 1e-14:
            y = a.copy()
        else:
            y = y / ys

        c = convolve_sequence(y)
        g = active_gradient(y, c, k_count, 0.4 + 1.6 * frac)

        trial = project_simplex(y - step * g)
        sc = score_normalized(trial)

        move = 0.0
        if np.all(np.isfinite(trial)):
            move = float(np.linalg.norm(trial - a))

        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < score - 1.0e-9:
                accept = True
            elif temp > 1.0e-7 and sc < score + temp and move > 1.0e-7:
                accept = True

        if accept:
            a_prev = a
            a = trial
            score = sc
            if sc < best_score:
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_score = sc
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
            rejects = 0
            step = min(float(max_step), step * 1.01)
            temp *= 0.9995
        else:
            rejects += 1
            no_improve += 1
            step *= 0.7
            if step < 1.0e-5 or rejects > 6:
                sigma = (0.05 + 0.05 * frac) / max(1.0, math.sqrt(float(n)))
                perturbed = best_a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                perturbed = np.maximum(perturbed, 0.0)
                perturbed = normalize(perturbed)
                new_score = score_normalized(perturbed)
                if np.isfinite(new_score):
                    a = perturbed
                    score = new_score
                a_prev = a.copy()
                t = 1.0
                step = float(base_step)
                rejects = 0
            else:
                a_prev = a.copy()
                t = 1.0

        it += 1
        if it % 2500 == 0 and time.monotonic() - last_print > 12.0:
            print("    optimizer it=%d best=%.7f cur=%.7f step=%.5f k=%d" % (
                it, best_score, score, step, k_count
            ))
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_score = score

    return best_a, best_score


def finalize_sequence(seq):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        arr = np.ones(4806, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(4806, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = arr.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        arr = np.ones(4806, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    arr = arr / s
    out = []
    for v in arr:
        out.append(float(v))
    return out


def verify_sequence(seq):
    try:
        lst = []
        for v in seq:
            lst.append(float(v))
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        return val
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def fallback_sequence():
    return [1.0 for _ in range(4806)]


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        total_start = time.monotonic()
        try:
            budget = float(budget_s)
        except Exception:
            budget = 2400.0
        if budget <= 0 or not np.isfinite(budget):
            budget = 2400.0
        deadline = total_start + max(120.0, budget - 30.0)

        rng = np.random.default_rng(2024121)
        n_final = 8192
        given = get_initial_sequence()

        print("=== propose_candidate start ===")
        print("budget=%.1f final_n=%d given_len=%s" % (
            budget, n_final, len(given) if given is not None else "none"
        ))

        cand_n = []
        make_candidates(n_final, given, rng, cand_n)
        top_initial = choose_top(cand_n, 8)

        best_seq = None
        best_score = float("inf")
        if top_initial:
            best_seq = top_initial[0][2].copy()
            best_score = top_initial[0][0]
            print("initial best score=%.7f name=%s" % (best_score, top_initial[0][1]))

        final_candidates = []
        if given is not None and len(given) <= 20000:
            final_candidates.append(("given_orig", given))

        m_small = 128
        small_best = None
        small_best_score = float("inf")

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        small_budget = 0.0
        if remaining > 1500:
            small_budget = 260.0
        elif remaining > 1000:
            small_budget = 160.0
        elif remaining > 700:
            small_budget = 90.0

        if small_budget > 40.0:
            print("small profile search budget=%.0f" % small_budget)
            small_start = time.monotonic()
            small_cands = []
            make_candidates(m_small, given, rng, small_cands)

            de_budget = max(0.0, min(120.0, small_budget * 0.45))
            seeds = (11, 23)
            si = 0
            for sd in seeds:
                if time.monotonic() > small_start + de_budget:
                    break
                a = de_shape_search(m_small, de_budget, sd)
                if a is not None:
                    add_candidate(small_cands, "de_%d" % sd, a)
                si += 1

            small_top = choose_top(small_cands, 5)
            if small_top:
                profiles = []
                for item in small_top:
                    profiles.append(item[2])

                left_small = max(0.0, small_budget - (time.monotonic() - small_start))
                if left_small > 10.0:
                    small_best, small_best_score = small_refine(profiles, m_small, left_small)
                    if small_best is not None:
                        print("small profile best score=%.7f" % small_best_score)

            if small_best is not None:
                small_up = upsample_profile(small_best, n_final)
                add_candidate(cand_n, "small_up", small_up)
                final_candidates.append(("small_orig", small_best))

        # Blends between the original (if available) and the best small profile.
        if given is not None and small_best is not None:
            given_up = upsample_profile(given, n_final)
            small_up = upsample_profile(small_best, n_final)
            for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
                blend = float(alpha) * given_up + (1.0 - float(alpha)) * small_up
                add_candidate(cand_n, "blend_%d" % int(round(100 * float(alpha))), blend)

        top_initial = choose_top(cand_n, 10)
        if top_initial:
            cur = top_initial[0]
            if np.isfinite(cur[0]) and cur[0] < best_score:
                best_seq = cur[2].copy()
                best_score = cur[0]
                print("updated initial best=%.7f name=%s" % (best_score, cur[1]))

        sorted_cands = []
        for item in cand_n:
            if np.isfinite(item[0]):
                sorted_cands.append(item)
        sorted_cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)

        start_items = []
        if sorted_cands:
            append_unique_start(start_items, sorted_cands[0])

        fallback_names = ("given", "small", "rand", "log", "step", "sin", "pow", "blend")
        for nm in fallback_names:
            for item in sorted_cands:
                if item[1].startswith(nm):
                    append_unique_start(start_items, item)
                    if len(start_items) >= 3:
                        break
            if len(start_items) >= 3:
                break

        for item in sorted_cands:
            append_unique_start(start_items, item)
            if len(start_items) >= 3:
                break

        start_items = start_items[:3]
        random_pref = start_items[1] if len(start_items) > 1 else None

        # Prefer diverse starts: best, a random/smooth start if present, second best.
        ordered_starts = []
        if start_items:
            append_unique_start(ordered_starts, start_items[0])
        if len(start_items) >= 2:
            append_unique_start(ordered_starts, start_items[1])
        if len(start_items) >= 3:
            append_unique_start(ordered_starts, start_items[2])
        for item in start_items:
            append_unique_start(ordered_starts, item)
            if len(ordered_starts) >= 3:
                break
        start_items = ordered_starts[:3]

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 180.0 and start_items:
            if len(start_items) == 1:
                fracs = (0.92,)
            elif len(start_items) == 2:
                fracs = (0.58, 0.40)
            else:
                fracs = (0.52, 0.28, 0.20)

            total_rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            for i, item in enumerate(start_items):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
                    break
                frac = fracs[i] if i < len(fracs) else 0.1
                dur = total_rem * frac
                dur = min(dur, deadline - time.monotonic())
                if dur < 60.0:
                    dur = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if dur < 30.0:
                        break
                print("full optimization start i=%d name=%s dur=%.0f" % (i, item[1], dur))
                res, rs = run_optimizer(item[2], dur, rng, base_step=0.45, max_step=1.4, k_min=4, k_max=64)
                print("full optimization result i=%d score=%.7f" % (i, rs))
                if np.isfinite(rs) and rs < best_score:
                    best_seq = res.copy()
                    best_score = rs

            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if remaining > 90.0 and best_seq is not None:
                print("final polish budget=%.0f" % remaining)
                res, rs = run_optimizer(best_seq, remaining, rng, base_step=0.12, max_step=0.6, k_min=8, k_max=80)
                print("polish score=%.7f" % rs)
                if np.isfinite(rs) and rs < best_score:
                    best_seq = res.copy()
                    best_score = rs

        if best_seq is not None:
            final_candidates.append(("global_best", best_seq))

        # Add a few strong initial candidates for final direct verification.
        for item in top_initial[:3]:
            final_candidates.append((item[1], item[2]))

        # De-duplicate and verify a small number of candidates with the official evaluator.
        seen = []
        for nm, seq in final_candidates:
            if seq is None:
                continue
            arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size == 0:
                continue
            duplicate = False
            for prev_nm, prev_arr in seen:
                if arr.size == len(prev_arr):
                    if np.max(np.abs(arr - prev_arr)) < 1e-5:
                        duplicate = True
                        break
            if not duplicate:
                seen.append((nm, arr))
                if len(seen) >= 4:
                    break

        best_final_seq = None
        best_final_val = float("inf")
        for nm, arr in seen:
            val = verify_sequence(arr)
            print("verify %s score=%.7f" % (nm, val))
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_final_val:
                best_final_val = val
                best_final_seq = arr

        if best_final_seq is None:
            if best_seq is not None:
                best_final_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
            elif given is not None:
                best_final_seq = np.asarray(given, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                best_final_seq = np.ones(4806, dtype=np.float64)

        result = finalize_sequence(best_final_seq)
        print("=== returning length=%d final_score_reported=%.7f ===" % (len(result), best_final_val))
        return result

    except Exception as exc:
        print("Fatal error in propose_candidate:", exc)
        try:
            g = get_initial_sequence()
            if g is not None and g.size > 0:
                return finalize_sequence(g)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return finalize_sequence(fallback_sequence())
```
